def convolve_full(a,b):
    n=len(a), m=len(b)
    size = next_power_of_two(n+m-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size), fb=..., return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:n+m-1]

Let's design mirror descent update details:
def update(p, target_M, alpha):
   q = convolve_fft(p,p)
   M = target_M
   r = np.maximum(q - M, 0)
   # optional r **= 2? Use r maybe.
   if r.sum() <= 1e-30: return p, q, q.max(), False
   # cross = coefficient ∑ r_k p_{k-i}
   cross = cross_corr(r,p) # length n
   ng = p * cross
   maxng = ng.max()
   if maxng <= 0: return ...
   x = alpha * ng / maxng
   # To avoid underflow, clip x <= 50? alpha likely <5.
   p_new = p * np.exp(-x)
   # Add tiny uniform to avoid zeros? eps = 1e-12 / n maybe.
   p_new += 1e-12 / n
   s=p_new.sum(); if s<=0 invalid
   p_new /= s
   q_new = convolve_fft(p_new,p_new) # maybe cache if evaluating score
   return p_new, q_new, q_new.max(), True

Convolution helper:
def convolve_fft(a, b):
    if a.size ==0 or b.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    size = 1
    target = a.size + b.size -1
    while size < target: size <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, size)[:target]
    # numerical tiny imag removed by irfft; round negative near zero? For max, negative irrelevant.

Perturbation function:
def random_perturb(p, rng, strength):
   n=p.size
   # smooth noise: generate FFT random with decay
   freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
   spec = (rng.standard_normal(n//2+1) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(n//2+1))
   # zero out DC, low-pass
   spec *= np.exp(- (freqs / 0.2)**2)
   noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
   s = noise.std()
   if s>0:
       p2 = p * np.exp(strength * noise / s)
   p2 += 1e-12/n
   return p2/p2.sum()

Let's consider target from m-level equal blocks. We can solve via continuous calculus maybe. Suppose m blocks equal length 1/m, values v_j (j=0..m-1), mean E[v]=m (to normalize density: sum v_j/m=1). The maximum often occurs at interior of block pairs. We can formulate h as:
For t ∈ [0,2], there exist i,j such that intervals overlap. Since step function constant, h(t) = ∑_{i,j} v_i v_j * overlap_length(i,j,t) where overlap length is linear in t between breakpoints at (a-b)/m. Thus h is piecewise quadratic? Let's detail code structure.

Top-level helpers:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def next_pow2(x):
    s=1
    while s<x: s <<=1
    return s

def convolve_fft(a,b):
    if a.size==0 or b.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    need = a.size + b.size -1
    s=next_pow2(need)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,s)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,s)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,s)[:need]
    return out

def self_conv_fft(p):
    s=next_pow2(2*p.size-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,s)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,s)[:2*p.size-1]
    return out

def cross_corr_fft(r,p):
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   need=3*n-2
   s=next_pow2(need)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,s)
   fr=np.fft.rfft(r[::-1],s)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fr,s)[:need]
   L=2*n-2
   return out[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
```
Check cross length: r length 2n-1. Need 3n-2. If n=1, r length1, need=1, L=0, out[0:1][::-1]. Good.

Normalize:
```
def renorm(p):
   p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
   p = np.where(p>0,p,0.0)  # no loop
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 0: return p
   return p/s
```
Need no lambda. Use np.clip(p,0.0,None).

Approx score:
```
def approx_score(p):
   q=self_conv_fft(p)
   if q.size==0: return np.inf
   return float(2.0*p.size*max(0.0, float(q.max())))
```
Assumes sum1. If p not normalized, need divide by sum^2? We normalize before. For init, normalize.

Initial tiles:
```
def tile_levels(n,m,first,last):
   arr=np.empty(n)
   if m<=1:
      arr.fill(first)
      return arr
   for j in range(m):
      a=(j*n)//m
      b=((j+1)*n)//m
      if a>=b: continue
      val = first + (last-first)*j/float(m-1)
      arr[a:b]=val
   return arr
```
No lambda. For n=0? n>0.

def improve(p, seed, max_time):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  n=p.size
  p = renorm(p)
  q = self_conv_fft(p); cur=q.max()
  best_p=p.copy(); best_cur=cur
  alpha=0.3
  target_floor = 1.50274349/(2*n)  # exact constant accessible? use 1.50274349
  floor = target_floor
  no_progress=0
  while time < deadline:
    q = self_conv_fft(p) if q not current? We maintain q after accepted.
    cur = float(q.max())
    # ensure q nonneg small
    if cur <= floor:
       # try lower floor gradually if time
       new_floor = max(floor*0.995, floor - 1e-7/n)
       if new_floor < cur: floor = new_floor
       # else maybe use cur - 2%
    M = max(floor, cur - 0.05*cur)  # at least 5% reduction target
    r = q - M
    r = np.where(r>0,r,0.0)
    if r.sum() <= 1e-30:
       # lower floor or break
       if floor > 0.5*cur: floor=0.5*cur
       else: break? continue with random
    g = cross_corr_fft(r,p)
    ng = p*g
    maxng = ng.max()
    if maxng <=0: random perturb
    # adaptive alpha
    accepted=False
    for a in [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*0.1]:
       x = a*ng/maxng
       x = np.minimum(x, 30.0)
       p_try = p * np.exp(-x)
       p_try += 1e-12/n
       s=p_try.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       p_try /= s
       q_try = self_conv_fft(p_try)
       qmax_try=float(q_try.max())
       if np.isfinite(qmax_try) and qmax_try < cur - 1e-12:
           accept; break
       # also if qmax_try < -? no
    if not accepted:
       # Try random perturbation around p
       p_try = random_perturb(p, rng, 0.1)
       q_try=... if improve accept
       if not: alpha=max(0.05, alpha*0.7); no_progress+=1; if no_progress>50: p=best_p.copy(); alpha=0.2; floor=max(floor, best_cur*0.95)?
    if accepted:
       if qmax < best_cur: best_p=p.copy(); best_cur=qmax
       alpha=min(1.0, alpha*1.05)
       no_progress=0
       q = q_try; p = p_try
  return best_p, best_cur, q?

Random perturb: Need function.
```
def random_perturb(p, rng, strength):
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p.copy()
    # low-pass random FFT
    size=n
    spec = rng.standard_normal(size//2+1) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(size//2+1)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(size)
    # zeros DC
    spec[0]=0.0
    spec *= np.exp(-4.0*(freq* size / 10.0)**2)  # low pass
    noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, size)
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise)))
    if sd <= 0: 
       noise = rng.standard_normal(size); sd=1.0
    p2 = p * np.exp(strength * noise / sd)
    p2 = np.clip(p2,0.0,None)+1e-12/n
    s=p2.sum()
    if s<=0: return p.copy()
    return p2/s
```
No lambda.
Maybe also block perturb:
```
def block_perturb(p,rng):
   n=p.size; c=p.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//50)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
   c[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1))
   if rng.random()<0.5: c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
   return renorm(c)
```
This uses top-level.

def refine_sequence(p, seed, stop_time):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=p.size
    p=renorm(p)
    q = self_conv_fft(p); cur = max(0.0, float(q.max()))
    best = p.copy(); best_cur = cur; best_q = q.copy()
    alpha = 0.25
    frac = 0.90
    no_progress = 0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
        qmax = cur
        if qmax <= best_cur * 0.999999 and no_progress > 50:
            p = best.copy(); q = best_q.copy(); cur = best_cur
        M = frac * qmax
        # ensure M positive finite
        r = q - M
        np.clip(r, 0.0, None, out=r)  # can use out to save memory? r copy? We can allocate r = np.maximum(q - M, 0.0)
        if not np.isfinite(r).all() or r.max() <= 0.0:
             # random perturb or break
        g = cross_corr_fft(r,p)
        g = np.clip(g,0.0,None)
        ng = p*g
        maxng = float(ng.max())
        if maxng <= 0.0 or not finite: random perturb
        else:
            for a in (alpha, 0.5*alpha, 0.25*alpha):
                 x = a*ng/maxng; x = np.minimum(x, 30.0)
                 p_try = p*np.exp(-x); p_try += 1e-14/n; p_try/=p_try.sum()
                 q_try = self_conv_fft(p_try); qmax_try=float(q_try.max())
                 if finite and qmax_try < cur - 1e-14: accept
            if accepted: ...
        if not accepted:
             # random perturb tries
             for strength in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5): ...
             if accepted: ...
             else:
                no_progress +=1
                if no_progress > 20: frac=max(0.7,frac-0.02)
                if no_progress > 100: alpha=max(0.05, alpha*0.8); if time since last_perturb >? p=random stronger
                if no_progress > 500: break
    return best,best_cur
```

Candidate maker code:
```
def make_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # levels
    specs=(2,2.0,1.0),(4,3.0,0.8),(8,4.0,0.6),(16,5.0,0.5),(32,6.0,0.4)
    for m,f,l in specs: starts.append(tile_levels(n,m,f,l))
    # exponentials
    for lam in (0.6,1.0,1.6,2.4): starts.append(np.exp(-lam*x))
    # powers
    for a in (0.3,0.6,1.0,2.0): starts.append(np.power(1.0-x,a))
    # inverse
    for lam in (1.0,2.0): starts.append(1.0/(1.0+lam*x))
    # random smooth
    for k in range(3):
       p=renorm(np.ones(n) * (1.0 + 0.3*random_lowpass_noise(n, rng)))
       starts.append(p)
```

```
def smooth_noise(n, rng):
   spec = rng.standard_normal(n//2+1) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(n//2+1)
   freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
   band=max(1,n//20)
   spec *= np.exp(-((freq*n)/band)**2)
   spec[0]=0.0
   return np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
```

Implementation details for `renorm`:
```
def renorm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(p.shape, 1.0/max(1,p.size)) if p.size else np.ones(1)
    return p/s
```
If p.size 0 not; but have n>0.

For q2=p0p2+p1p1? full: q2 = p0p2 + p1p1 + p2p0 =2p0p2+p1^2. derivative p0=2p2; cross gives p2. So gradient is 2*cross except when k=2i? Still two terms but if j=i and j=i same? q_{2i} includes p_i^2 once? Wait full convolution sum over all ordered pairs (j,k-j) with both in 0..n-1. For k=2i, terms j=i and j=i are the same ordered pair? In the sum over j, j=i appears once, so derivative of p_i^2 is 2p_i. Cross gives p_i. Missing factor2. So gradient=2*cross always? Factor 2 irrelevant.

But in q_k, for off-diagonal, there are two ordered pairs (i,k-i) and (k-i,i). They correspond to different j in sum; derivative wrt p_i gets contributions from both j values, hence 2p. Good.

```

def make_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    if n <=1:
       return [np.ones(n)]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    specs=(2,2.0,1.0),(4,3.0,0.8),(8,4.0,0.6),(16,5.0,0.5),(32,6.0,0.4)
    for spec in specs:
       arr=tile_levels(n,spec[0],spec[1],spec[2])
       starts.append(arr)
    lams=(0.6,1.0,1.6,2.4)
    for lam in lams:
       starts.append(np.exp(-lam*x))
    pows=(0.3,0.6,1.0,2.0)
    one_minus=1.0-x
    for a in pows:
       starts.append(np.power(one_minus,a))
    for lam in (1.0,2.0):
       starts.append(1.0/(1.0+lam*x))
    # Add one random lowpass
    for k in range(2):
       noise=smooth_noise(n,rng)
       sd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise)))
       if sd>0:
           arr=np.exp(0.3*noise/sd)
       else:
           arr=np.ones(n)
       starts.append(arr)
    # normalize distinct
    out=[]
    seen=[]? Not necessary but can avoid duplicates? We'll just normalize all finite.
    for arr in starts:
       arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
       arr[np.isnan]=... use np.isfinite? `arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)`
       arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
       s=float(arr.sum())
       if s>0 and np.isfinite(s): out.append(arr/s)
    return out
```
Need avoid loop over array setting nan? fine.

`smooth_noise` uses FFT. For n large, two normals arrays length n//2+1 (complex creation). Good.

`random_perturb`: Use smooth_noise, but if p small? exponent strength*noise/sd; p entries ~1/n. If noise std1, strength 0.5, exp variations moderate. Add uniform. If strength 1.0, exp range e±large. Use strength up to 0.7.

`block_perturb`: Use rng int; for n small width. If start+width > n adjust.
```
def block_perturb(p, rng):
    n=p.size
    c=p.copy()
    if n <=1: return c
    lo=max(1,n//300)
    hi=max(lo+1,n//20)
    width=int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1))
    if width>n: width=n
    start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
    c[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.15)))
    if rng.random()<0.5:
        c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    return renorm(c)
```
No lambda.

def refine_sequence(p, seed, stop_time):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=renorm(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64))
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.ones(1), np.inf
    q=self_conv_fft(p)
    cur=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur<0: cur=0.0; q=np.zeros_like(q)
    best=p.copy(); best_cur=cur; best_q=q.copy()
    alpha=0.25
    frac=0.88
    no_progress=0
    last_perturb=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 1.0:
        qmax=cur
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax<=0:
            best=p.copy(); break
        # if current much worse than best and idle, reset
        if best_cur < qmax * 0.999 and no_progress > 20:
            p=best.copy(); q=best_q.copy(); cur=best_cur
        M=frac*qmax
        r=q - M
        r=np.where(r>0.0, r, 0.0)
        accepted=False
        if float(r.max()) > 0.0:
            g=cross_corr_fft(r,p)
            g=np.where(g>0.0, g, 0.0)
            ng=p*g
            maxng=float(np.max(ng))
            if np.isfinite(maxng) and maxng>0.0:
                alphas=(alpha, 0.5*alpha, 0.25*alpha)
                best_trial_p=None; best_trial_q=cur; best_trial_qarr=None
                for a in alphas:
                    if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5: break
                    x = a * ng / maxng
                    np.clip(x, 0.0, 30.0, out=x)  # x array? Ensure x is array writable. x = a*ng/maxng creates float array. clip out okay.
                    p_try = p * np.exp(-x)
                    p_try += 1e-14/float(n)
                    s=float(p_try.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: continue
                    p_try /= s
                    q_try = self_conv_fft(p_try)
                    qmax_try = float(np.max(q_try)) if q_try.size else np.inf
                    if np.isfinite(qmax_try):
                        if qmax_try < best_trial_q:
                            best_trial_p=p_try; best_trial_q=qmax_try; best_trial_qarr=q_try
                if best_trial_p is not None and best_trial_q < cur - 1e-14:
                    p=best_trial_p; q=best_trial_qarr; cur=best_trial_q; accepted=True
                    if cur < best_cur:
                        best=p.copy(); best_cur=cur; best_q=q.copy()
                    no_progress=0
                    alpha=min(0.8, alpha*1.05)
                    frac=min(0.95, frac+0.002)
        if not accepted:
            # random perturb tries
            candidates=[]
            candidates.append((random_perturb(p,rng,0.05),0.0))
            candidates.append((random_perturb(p,rng,0.15),0.0))
            candidates.append((block_perturb(p,rng),0.0))
            # If no progress high, stronger
            if no_progress > 30:
                candidates.append((random_perturb(p,rng,0.5),0.0))
            for p_try, dummy in candidates:
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5: break
                q_try=self_conv_fft(p_try)
                qmax_try=float(np.max(q_try)) if q_try.size else np.inf
                if np.isfinite(qmax_try) and qmax_try < cur - 1e-14:
                    p=p_try; q=q_try; cur=qmax_try; accepted=True; break
            if accepted:
                if cur < best_cur:
                    best=p.copy(); best_cur=cur; best_q=q.copy()
                no_progress=0
                alpha=min(0.8, alpha*1.05)
            else:
                no_progress += 1
                if no_progress > 10:
                    frac=max(0.75, frac-0.01)
                if no_progress > 50:
                    alpha=max(0.05, alpha*0.8)
                if no_progress > 200:
                    # revert and stronger perturbation once
                    p=best.copy(); q=best_q.copy(); cur=best_cur
                    ptry=random_perturb(p,rng,1.0)
                    qtry=self_conv_fft(ptry); qm=float(np.max(qtry))
                    if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < cur - 1e-14:
                        p=ptry; q=qtry; cur=qm; no_progress=0
                    else:
                        no_progress=0 # break soon?
                if no_progress > 600:
                    break
    # ensure best finite
    if not np.isfinite(best_cur):
        best=renorm(np.ones(n)); best_cur=2.0/n? Actually qmax for uniform =? for n p uniform qmax=1/n; approx_score=2.
        q=self_conv_fft(best); best_cur=float(np.max(q))
    return best, best_cur
```

Let's draft final code in my head:

```
import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_SCORE = 1.50274349

def next_pow2(x):
    s=1
    while s < x:
        s <<= 1
    return s

def renorm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size ==0:
       return np.ones(1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       return np.full(p.shape, 1.0/float(p.size))
    return p / s

def self_conv_fft(p):
    p = renorm(p)? No, self_conv should not renorm? In search p normalized. But if called with approx from make starts normalized. To avoid side effects, don't renorm inside; but approximate_score may call after renorm. Better not renorm to catch? But if p not normalized, q scale changes; approx_score should normalize. We'll have approx_score renorm.
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    need = 2*n -1
    s = next_pow2(need)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, s)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, s)[:need]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    return out

def approx_score(p):
    p=renorm(p)
    q=self_conv_fft(p)
    if q.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*p.size*float(np.max(q)))
```
`renorm` in approx_score recomputes normalization; p already maybe. Fine.

`cross_corr_fft` expects p nonnegative not necessarily normalized, r length 2n-1.
```
def cross_corr_fft(r,p):
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 3*n - 2
    s = next_pow2(need)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,s)
    fr=np.fft.rfft(r[::-1], s)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fr, s)[:need]
    L=2*n-2
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    g = out[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    return g
```
If n=1, L=0, slice 0:1 ok.

`tile_levels` if m>n? We can set m=min(m,n). If m==1 all first.
```
def tile_levels(n,m,first,last):
    if n<=0: return np.ones(0)
    m = int(min(max(1,m), n))
    arr=np.empty(n)
    for j in range(m):
        a=(j*n)//m
        b=((j+1)*n)//m
        if a>=b: continue
        if m==1:
           val=first
        else:
           val = first + (last-first)*float(j)/float(m-1)
        arr[a:b]=val
    # if some unfilled? for j=m-1 b=n, all filled.
    return arr
```
If b could be n for last, okay.

`smooth_noise`:
```
def smooth_noise(n,rng):
    size=max(1,int(n))
    spec = rng.standard_normal(size//2+1) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(size//2+1)
    if size==1:
        return np.zeros(1)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(size)
    band=max(1.0, size/20.0)
    decay = np.exp(-((freq*size)/band)**2)
    spec = spec * decay
    spec[0]=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, size)
    noise=np.where(np.isfinite(noise), noise, 0.0)
    return noise
```
For size=1, rfftfreq length1, spec length1. We can handle generally: if spec.size>0 spec[0]=0; irfft length1 zero. okay.

`make_starts`:
```
def make_starts(n, rng):
    if n <=1:
        return [np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    out=[]
    specs=((2,2.0,1.0),(4,3.0,0.8),(8,4.0,0.6),(16,5.0,0.5),(32,6.0,0.4))
    for spec in specs:
       out.append(tile_levels(n,spec[0],spec[1],spec[2]))
    for lam in (0.6,1.0,1.6,2.4):
       out.append(np.exp(-lam*x))
    y=1.0-x
    for a in (0.3,0.6,1.0,2.0):
       out.append(np.power(y,a))
    for lam in (1.0,2.0):
       out.append(1.0/(1.0+lam*x))
    for k in range(2):
       noise=smooth_noise(n,rng)
       sd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise)))
       if sd>0.0:
           arr=np.exp(0.3*noise/sd)
       else:
           arr=np.ones(n)
       out.append(arr)
    res=[]
    for arr in out:
       arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
       arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
       s=float(arr.sum())
       if np.isfinite(s) and s>0.0:
           res.append(arr/s)
    if not res:
       res.append(np.full(n,1.0/float(n)))
    return res
```

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(p, rng, strength):
    n=p.size
    if n <=1:
        return p.copy()
    noise=smooth_noise(n,rng)
    sd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise)))
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <=0.0:
        noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
        sd=1.0
    p_try=p*np.exp(strength*noise/sd)
    p_try=np.where(np.isfinite(p_try), p_try, 0.0)
    p_try=np.clip(p_try,0.0,None)
    p_try = p_try + 1e-10/float(n)
    s=float(p_try.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
        return p.copy()
    return p_try/s
```
`block_perturb` similar.

def refine_sequence(p, seed, stop_time):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=renorm(p)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.ones(1), np.inf
    q=self_conv_fft(p)
    cur=float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur <=0:
       q=np.zeros_like(q); cur=0.0
    best=p.copy(); best_cur=cur; best_q=q.copy()
    alpha=0.25
    frac=0.88
    target_M = TARGET_SCORE/(2.0*float(n))
    no_progress=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 1.5:
       qmax=cur
       if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <=0.0:
          break
       if best_cur < qmax * 0.999 and no_progress > 20:
          p=best.copy(); q=best_q.copy(); cur=best_cur
       M_top = frac * qmax
       if target_M >0 and qmax > 1.15 * target_M:
           M = min(M_top, target_M)
       else:
           M = M_top
       r = q - M
       r = np.where(r > 0.0, r, 0.0)
       accepted=False
       if float(np.max(r)) > 0.0:
           g = cross_corr_fft(r,p)
           ng = p*g
           maxng = float(np.max(ng))
           if np.isfinite(maxng) and maxng > 0.0:
              trial_p=None; trial_q=cur; trial_qarr=None
              for a in (alpha, 0.5*alpha, 0.25*alpha):
                 if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5: break

                 p_try = np.where(np.isfinite(p_try), p_try, 0.0)

                     best=p.copy(); best_cur=cur; best_q=q.copy()
                  no_progress=0
                  alpha=min(0.7, alpha*1.05)
                  frac=min(0.95, frac+0.002)

          p_try = random_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
          q_try=self_conv_fft(p_try); qmax_try=float(np.max(q_try))
          if np.isfinite(qmax_try) and qmax_try < cur - 1e-14:
             ...

             p_try = random_perturb(p, rng, 0.2)
             ...
          else? can't nested else; use function? We can loop over strengths.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    try:
       np.set_num_threads(1)
    except Exception:
       pass
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)
    parent = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if arr_obj is not None:
        parent = np.asarray(arr_obj, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1:
            parent = parent.reshape(-1) if parent.size else parent
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(parent.size)
    if float(parent.sum()) < 0.01:
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # normalize fallback for potential return? evaluator scale invariant, but for safety
    fallback_raw = parent.copy()
    fallback = renorm(fallback_raw)
    try:
        best_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        best_exact = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        fallback = renorm(np.ones(n))
        try: best_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback.tolist()))
        except Exception: pass
    best_exact_p = fallback.copy()
    # make starts
    starts = make_starts(n, rng)
    # score candidates approximately
    scores=[]
    for st in starts:
       sc=approx_score(st)
       scores.append(sc if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf)
    orders = np.argsort(np.asarray(scores, dtype=np.float64))
    top=[]
    for idx in orders:
       if len(top)>=3: break
       top.append(starts[int(idx)])
    if not top:
       top.append(renorm(np.ones(n)))
    # exact evaluate top starts
    for p0 in top:
       try:
          sc=float(evaluate_sequence(p0.tolist()))
       except Exception:
          sc=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
          best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=renorm(p0)
    # refine loop
    num=len(top)
    for idx in range(num):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90: break
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0
       if remaining <= 20.0: break
       slice_duration = max(60.0, remaining / float(num - idx))
       stop = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + slice_duration)
       p_ref, score_ref = refine_sequence(top[idx], int(seed) + 1000*idx + 17, stop)
       if p_ref is not None and p_ref.size == n:
           try:
              sc=float(evaluate_sequence(p_ref.tolist()))
           except Exception:
              sc=np.inf
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
               best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=renorm(p_ref)
           # keep p_ref if approx better? not needed.
    # final refine of best exact if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
       p_ref, score_ref = refine_sequence(renorm(best_exact_p), int(seed)+9999, deadline-30.0)
       if p_ref is not None and p_ref.size == n:
           try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(p_ref.tolist()))
           except Exception: sc=np.inf
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
               best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=renorm(p_ref)
    # final ensure
    final_p = renorm(best_exact_p)
    # If final evaluation fails or worse? We know best_exact for best_exact_p. But final renorm may exact same.
    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```

This can be very effective. Let's implement a block optimizer:
- Choose m (e.g., 32). v length m positive, normalized mean1.
- Score R(v) = max_k C_k where C_k = (1/m)∑_{i=0}^{k-1} v_i v_{k-1-i} for k=1..2m (with valid indices). There are 2m breakpoints. (K=1..2m; i=0..m-1, j=K-1-i in range). We can compute for all k efficiently O(m^2) by nested loops adding to C[k]. For m=64, O4096 trivial.
- Minimize R(v). This is nonconvex? C_k quadratic indefinite? But small.
- Use a simple multiplicative descent similar to full but on block variables, using active K. We can optimize to near continuous m-block optimum.
- Since m small, we can even use scipy.optimize.minimize method='Nelder-Mead' if available, with parameter log v. But score nonsmooth max of quadratics; Nelder-Mead 32 dim maybe many eval but O4096 each; 10k eval ~40M operations in Python loops maybe okay but could be slow if Python nested. We can compute C with vectorization O(m^2) using np? Use np.convolve(v, v) gives coeff of polynomial; C_k = (1/m) conv[v][k-1]? Wait convolution (m length) full length 2m-1: conv[j]=∑_{i=0}^j v_i v_{j-i} for j=0..2m-2. Our k = j+1, so C_k=(1/m)conv[k-1]. Exactly! Because j=K-1. Great. So R(v)=max(np.convolve(v,v)) / m. Thus optimizing m-block score is minimize max(np.convolve(v,v)) subject to mean1. This is the same problem of length m but with scaling? Wait if we find an optimized sequence v of length m for the m-point problem, the score of v as length m is 2*R (since sum=m). If v is smoothed, tiling to n gives continuous step approx with same R. Maybe the optimal for large n is approximated by an optimized small sequence v of length M (e.g., 64 or 128) and tiling. The AC1 bound for step function with M blocks equals the score of the M-point sequence v with scaling? Yes if v is raw values and n=M? For v (length M) normalized sum=M? Score=2M*max(conv(v,v))/M^2 =2*maxconv/M. Tiled n with density v/mean gives same 2*maxconv/M. So we can solve the smaller problem exactly (length M) and tile to n, preserving approximate score. The finite n discrete differences O(1/M) can be corrected by full refinement.

This suggests a hierarchical approach: Use full algorithm on length m=128 (or 256) to find a good v, then tile to n and refine full. For m small, direct np.convolve in local search is cheap, can run many iterations. Could even do global random search to find near low score.

What is the asymptotic optimum for block count m? As m increases, the tile score approaches continuous optimum for that shape? Actually optimizing discrete length m directly gives score for a step function with m points, not continuous blocks. Tiling that discrete v gives continuous block density with values v; the continuous max at breakpoints corresponds to conv(v) scaled as above. The score of v as discrete length m is exactly 2M*maxconv/M^2 (if sum_v=M). This equals continuous tile score. Thus solving length m exactly (discrete) yields tile score equal to its own score. So we can use our full algorithm on m=128 (small) to get v with score S_m. As m increases, S_m should tend to the continuous optimum (infinite block). We can tile to n.

This is a powerful and compact strategy:
1. Choose M = maybe min(256, max(16, n//?))? The problem length n=6733; M=128 or 256. Solve optimization on M using direct convolution (O(M^2) very cheap) within a time slice.
2. Take best v, tile to length n (each v_i repeated L or ceil) and renormalize? Tiling raw v: sequence raw = np.repeat(v, L) with last adjust; normalize if needed; score invariant. This sequence is a step function. Its exact full score should close to score(v) maybe slightly different due n not multiple. Use evaluator to exact.
3. Then run full mirror descent on length n to polish finite-length effects and possibly exceed small-m approximation.

This is better than starting with arbitrary m levels. We can even run the full algorithm on a few small M values (64,128,256) with different seeds and tile best. Since M small, we can do more global random search.

    arr=np.empty(n)

        b=((j+1)*n)//m

def next_pow2(x):
    s=1
    while s < x: s <<=1
    return s

Let's implement `full_conv(a,b)` top-level. `self_conv_fft(p)` can call `full_conv(p,p)`. But cross needs p,r_rev.

```
def full_conv(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
    need=a.size+b.size-1
    if need==0: return empty
    if a.size < 128 and b.size < 128:
       out=np.convolve(a,b)
    else:
       s=next_pow2(need)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,s); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,s)
       out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,s)[:need]
    out=np.where(np.isfinite(out),out,0.0)
    return np.clip(out,0.0,None)
```
If b is r[::-1] negative stride, np.convolve handles? For small maybe. For FFT, rfft copies. Good.

return np.clip(g,0,None)

Top-level helper:
```
def top_starts(starts, k):
    scores=np.array([approx_score(s) for s in starts], dtype=float)
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    res=[]
    for idx in order:
       if len(res)>=k: break
       res.append(starts[int(idx)])
    return res
```

```
def tile_values(vals,n):
    m=vals.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)
    if m==1: return np.full(n, vals[0])
    arr=np.empty(n)
    for j in range(m):
       a=(j*n)//m; b=((j+1)*n)//m
       if a<b: arr[a:b]=vals[j]
    return arr
```

Potential syntax in refine_sequence: loops and variables. Let's write it cleanly:
```
def refine_sequence(p, seed, stop_time):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = renorm(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1), np.inf
    q = full_conv(p, p)
    cur = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros_like(q)
        cur = 0.0
    best = p.copy()
    best_cur = cur
    best_q = q.copy()
    alpha = 0.25
    frac = 0.88
    no_progress = 0
    target_m = TARGET_SCORE / (2.0 * float(n))
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 1.5:
        qmax = cur
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            break
        if best_cur < qmax * 0.999 and no_progress > 20:
            p = best.copy(); q = best_q.copy(); cur = best_cur
        m_thresh = frac * qmax
        if target_m > 0.0 and qmax > 1.15 * target_m:
            m_thresh = min(m_thresh, target_m)
        r = q - m_thresh
        r = np.where(r > 0.0, r, 0.0)
        accepted = False
        if float(np.max(r)) > 0.0:
            g = cross_corr(r, p)
            ng = p * g
            maxng = float(np.max(ng))
            scale = float(np.percentile(ng, 90)) if ng.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
                scale = maxng
            if np.isfinite(scale) and scale > 0.0:
                trial_p = None
                trial_q = cur
                trial_qarr = None
                for a in (alpha, 0.5 * alpha, 0.25 * alpha):
                    if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5:
                        break
                    x = a * ng / scale
                    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 30.0)
                    pt = p * np.exp(-x)
                    pt = np.where(np.isfinite(pt), pt, 0.0)
                    pt += 1e-10 / float(n)
                    s = float(pt.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    pt /= s
                    qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                    qm = float(np.max(qt))
                    if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < trial_q:
                        trial_p = pt
                        trial_q = qm
                        trial_qarr = qt
                if trial_p is not None and trial_q < cur - 1e-14:
                    p = trial_p
                    q = trial_qarr
                    cur = trial_q
                    accepted = True
                    if cur < best_cur:
                        best = p.copy(); best_cur = cur; best_q = q.copy()
                    no_progress = 0
                    alpha = min(0.7, alpha * 1.05)
                    frac = min(0.95, frac + 0.002)
        if not accepted:
            cand_p = None
            cand_q = None
            cand_qm = cur
            strengths = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35)
            if no_progress > 40:
                strengths = (0.15, 0.35, 0.7)
            for st in strengths:
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5:
                    break
                pt = random_perturb(p, rng, st)
                qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                qm = float(np.max(qt))
                if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < cand_qm:
                    cand_p = pt; cand_q = qt; cand_qm = qm
                    if cand_qm < cur - 1e-14:
                        break
            if cand_p is None or cand_qm >= cur - 1e-14:
                pt = block_perturb(p, rng)
                qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                qm = float(np.max(qt))
                if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < cur - 1e-14:
                    cand_p = pt; cand_q = qt; cand_qm = qm
            if cand_p is not None:
                p = cand_p; q = cand_q; cur = cand_qm
                accepted = True
                if cur < best_cur:
                    best = p.copy(); best_cur = cur; best_q = q.copy()
                no_progress = 0
                alpha = min(0.7, alpha * 1.05)
            else:
                no_progress += 1
                if no_progress > 10:
                    frac = max(0.72, frac - 0.015)
                if no_progress > 60:
                    alpha = max(0.05, alpha * 0.8)
                if no_progress > 250:
                    p = best.copy(); q = best_q.copy(); cur = best_cur
                    no_progress = 0
                if no_progress > 800:
                    break
    if not np.isfinite(best_cur):
        best = renorm(np.ones(n))
        q = full_conv(best, best)
        best_cur = float(np.max(q))
    return best, best_cur
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_SCORE = 1.50274349

def next_pow2(x):
    s = 1
    while s < x:
        s <<= 1
    return s

def renorm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(p.shape, 1.0 / float(p.size))
    return p / s

def full_conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0)
    need = a.size + b.size - 1
    if a.size <= 256 and b.size <= 256:
        out = np.convolve(a, b)
    else:
        s = next_pow2(need)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, s)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, s)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, s)[:need]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, None)

def approx_score(p):
    p = renorm(p)
    q = full_conv(p, p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * p.size * float(np.max(q)))

def top_starts(starts, k):
    if not starts:
        return []
    scores = np.empty(len(starts), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(len(starts)):
        sc = approx_score(starts[i])
        scores[i] = sc if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf
    res = []
    for idx in np.argsort(scores):
        idx = int(idx)
        if 0 <= idx < len(starts):
            res.append(starts[idx])
        if len(res) >= k:
            break
    return res

def tile_levels(n, m, first, last):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    m = int(min(max(1, m), n))
    arr = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
        a = (j * n) // m
        b = ((j + 1) * n) // m
        if a >= b:
            continue
        val = first if m == 1 else first + (last - first) * float(j) / float(m - 1)
        arr[a:b] = val
    return arr

def tile_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    m = vals.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(vals[0]))
    arr = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
        a = (j * n) // m
        b = ((j + 1) * n) // m
        if a < b:
            arr[a:b] = vals[j]
    return arr

def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    size = max(1, int(n))
    half = size // 2 + 1
    spec = rng.standard_normal(half) + 1j * rng.standard_normal(half)
    if size == 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(size)
    band = max(1.0, size / 20.0)
    spec = spec * np.exp(-((freq * size) / band) ** 2)
    spec[0] = 0.0
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec, size)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)

def make_starts(n, rng):
    if n <= 1:
        return [renorm(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = 1.0 - x
    out = []
    for m, f, l in ((2, 2.0, 1.0), (4, 3.0, 0.8), (8, 4.0, 0.6),
                    (16, 5.0, 0.5), (32, 6.0, 0.4)):
        out.append(tile_levels(n, m, f, l))
    for lam in (0.6, 1.0, 1.8):
        out.append(np.exp(-lam * x))
    for a in (0.3, 0.7, 1.4):
        out.append(np.power(y, a))
    for lam in (1.0, 2.2):
        out.append(1.0 / (1.0 + lam * x))
    for k in range(2):
        noise = smooth_noise(n, rng)
        sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise * noise)))
        if sd > 0.0:
            out.append(np.exp(0.3 * noise / sd))
        else:
            out.append(np.ones(n))
    for k in range(2):
        arr = rng.random(n) * 0.7 + 0.1
        out.append(np.sort(arr)[::-1])
    res = []
    for arr in out:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            res.append(arr / s)
    if not res:
        res.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n)))
    return res

def random_perturb(p, rng, strength):
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    noise = smooth_noise(n, rng)
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise * noise)))
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <= 0.0:
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        sd = 1.0
    q = p * np.exp(strength * noise / sd)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    q += 1e-10 / float(n)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    return q / s

def block_perturb(p, rng):
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    q = p.copy()
    lo = max(1, n // 300)
    hi = max(lo + 1, n // 20)
    if hi > n:
        hi = n
    width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    q[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15)))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        q = 0.5 * (q + q[::-1])
    return renorm(q)

def cross_corr(r, p):
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    out = full_conv(p, r[::-1])
    if out.size < need:
        pad = np.zeros(max(0, need - out.size), dtype=np.float64)
        out = np.concatenate((out, pad))
    L = 2 * n - 2
    g = out[L - n + 1:L + 1][::-1]
    return np.clip(g, 0.0, None)

def refine_sequence(p, seed, stop_time):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = renorm(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1), np.inf
    q = full_conv(p, p)
    cur = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros_like(q)
        cur = 0.0
    best = p.copy()
    best_cur = cur
    best_q = q.copy()
    alpha = 0.25
    frac = 0.88
    no_progress = 0
    target_m = TARGET_SCORE / (2.0 * float(n))
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 1.5:
        qmax = cur
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            break
        if best_cur < qmax * 0.999 and no_progress > 20:
            p = best.copy()
            q = best_q.copy()
            cur = best_cur
        m_floor = max(target_m, 0.75 * qmax)
        m_thresh = min(frac * qmax, m_floor)
        r = q - m_thresh
        r = np.where(r > 0.0, r, 0.0)
        accepted = False
        if float(np.max(r)) > 0.0:
            g = cross_corr(r, p)
            ng = p * g
            maxng = float(np.max(ng))
            scale = float(np.percentile(ng, 90)) if ng.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
                scale = maxng
            if np.isfinite(scale) and scale > 0.0:
                tp = None
                tq = cur
                tqa = None
                for a in (alpha, 0.5 * alpha, 0.25 * alpha):
                    if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5:
                        break
                    x = a * ng / scale
                    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 30.0)
                    pt = p * np.exp(-x)
                    pt = np.where(np.isfinite(pt), pt, 0.0)
                    pt += 1e-10 / float(n)
                    s = float(pt.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    pt /= s
                    qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                    qm = float(np.max(qt))
                    if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < tq:
                        tp, tq, tqa = pt, qm, qt
                if tp is not None and tq < cur - 1e-14:
                    p, q, cur, accepted = tp, tqa, tq, True
                    if cur < best_cur:
                        best, best_cur, best_q = p.copy(), cur, q.copy()
                    no_progress = 0
                    alpha = min(0.7, alpha * 1.05)
                    frac = min(0.95, frac + 0.002)
        if not accepted:
            cp = None
            cq = None
            cqm = cur
            for st in (0.1, 0.3):
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1.5:
                    break
                pt = random_perturb(p, rng, st)
                qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                qm = float(np.max(qt))
                if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < cqm:
                    cp, cq, cqm = pt, qt, qm
                    if cqm < cur - 1e-14:
                        break
            if cp is None or cqm >= cur - 1e-14:
                pt = block_perturb(p, rng)
                qt = full_conv(pt, pt)
                qm = float(np.max(qt))
                if np.isfinite(qm) and qm < cur - 1e-14:
                    cp, cq, cqm = pt, qt, qm
            if cp is not None:
                p, q, cur, accepted = cp, cq, cqm, True
                if cur < best_cur:
                    best, best_cur, best_q = p.copy(), cur, q.copy()
                no_progress = 0
                alpha = min(0.7, alpha * 1.05)
            else:
                no_progress += 1
                if no_progress > 20:
                    frac = max(0.70, frac - 0.02)
                if no_progress > 50:
                    alpha = max(0.05, alpha * 0.8)
                if no_progress > 300:
                    break
    if not np.isfinite(best_cur):
        best = renorm(np.ones(n))
        q = full_conv(best, best)
        best_cur = float(np.max(q))
    return best, best_cur

def safe_exact(p):
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        sc = np.inf
    return sc

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    try:
        np.set_num_threads(1)
    except Exception:
        pass
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)

    parent = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is not None:
        parent = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1:
            parent = parent.reshape(-1)
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(parent.size)

    fallback = renorm(parent)
    if float(parent.sum()) < 0.01:
        fallback = renorm(np.ones(n))
    best_exact = safe_exact(fallback)
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        fallback = renorm(np.ones(n))
        best_exact = safe_exact(fallback)
    best_exact_p = fallback.copy()

    candidates = []
    dstarts = make_starts(n, rng)
    for d in top_starts(dstarts, 2):
        candidates.append(d)
        candidates.append(d[::-1])

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    small_allowed = min(300.0, 0.35 * remaining)
    if n > 512 and small_allowed > 80.0:
        small_stop = time.monotonic() + small_allowed
        for M in (32, 64, 128):
            if M > n:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() > small_stop - 20.0:
                break
            srng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000 + M)
            for i, sp in enumerate(top_starts(make_starts(M, srng), 2)):
                if time.monotonic() > small_stop - 5.0:
                    break
                stop = min(small_stop, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
                if stop < time.monotonic() + 5.0:
                    continue
                vp, _ = refine_sequence(sp, int(seed) + 20000 + M * 10 + i, stop)
                if vp is not None and vp.size == M:
                    tp = renorm(tile_values(vp, n))
                    candidates.append(tp)
                    candidates.append(tp[::-1])

    if not candidates:
        candidates.append(renorm(np.ones(n)))

    ftop = top_starts(candidates, 3)
    for p0 in ftop:
        sc = safe_exact(p0)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact, best_exact_p = sc, p0.copy()

    for i, p0 in enumerate(ftop):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0
        if remaining < 30.0:
            break
        stop = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, remaining / float(len(ftop) - i)))
        p_ref, _ = refine_sequence(p0, int(seed) + 50000 + i, stop)
        if p_ref is not None and p_ref.size == n:
            sc = safe_exact(p_ref)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact, best_exact_p = sc, p_ref.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        p_ref, _ = refine_sequence(renorm(best_exact_p), int(seed) + 88888, deadline - 15.0)
        if p_ref is not None and p_ref.size == n:
            sc = safe_exact(p_ref)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact, best_exact_p = sc, p_ref.copy()

    final_p = renorm(best_exact_p)
    if final_p.size == 0:
        final_p = np.ones(1)
    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
